# SGW_Ihpet_Complete_Dev_Master_v1

Markdown rendering of `SGW_Ihpet_Complete_Dev_Master_v1.xlsx` from the SGW Claude Server Handoff Pack v1.2.
Generated by `xlsx_to_md.py`; cell values only (no formulas, formatting or comments).
Sheet order and headers are preserved verbatim. Evidence labels inside the
sheets control authority, per the pack's `docs/SOURCE_POLICY.md`.

## 00_README

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Workbook | SGW Ihpet — Complete Mission / Dev Master v1 |
| Strict scope | All recovered Ihpet mission/runtime content: Crater Light SGU branch, Crater Dark Praxis branch, secret-Ihpet/resurrection interior, faction report split, later Return-to-Ihpet destruction arc, side missions, dialogues, NPC/enemies, items, props, Kismet and map evidence. |
| Map policy | Ihpet_Crater_Light and Ihpet_Crater_Dark are original recovered production maps and should be used directly. This workbook does NOT treat those maps as rebuild targets. |
| Missing maps | Ihpet_E1 World74 and Ihpet_E2 World75 survive as world records; E1 minimap survives, but production UMAP/MapData are missing. |
| SGU branch | 713→715→716→940→717→718→719→720; side941→942→943; then secret-Ihpet/resurrection and804→Omega. |
| Praxis branch | 714→960→961→962→963→964→966→967; side968→969→944; then secret-Ihpet/resurrection and805→Harset. |
| Ra Resurrected | 800→801→802→803 is the recovered resurrection sequence. Mission803 explicitly says Ra returns. |
| Later return | SGU806 vs Praxis807 → shared808-813 → SGU814 or Praxis815 → shared816 → SGU817 / Praxis818. Side819-821. |
| Alternate content | 1805 Descent into the Temple of Ihpet and1806 Ra's Reliquary are preserved as alternate/condensed paths because exact prerequisites/relationship to the primary chain are unresolved. |
| Evidence labels | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED; PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING; OUT OF SCOPE. |
| Authority rule | Raw cooked mission/dialog/world/gate data outrank progression and secondary sheets. Missing server logic remains explicit. |
